### [1] [Renormalizations in holomorphic field theories on Kähler manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00546)
*Minghao Wang,Junrong Yan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在闭实解析Kähler流形上的全纯场论中，Feynman图积分在热核、Cauchy主值和zeta函数三种重整化方案下均收敛且结果一致，其核心工具是代数几何中的‘奇妙紧化’理论，并由此导出相应的规范反常公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Feynman图积分的发散性是微扰量子场论的核心问题，需通过重整化予以处理；本文旨在为全纯场论在几何背景下的重整化提供严格、统一且几何可理解的框架。

Method: 运用代数几何中wonderful compactification理论对Feynman图积分的奇点结构进行几何紧化，结合热核、Cauchy主值与zeta函数三种重整化方案，在闭实解析Kähler流形上建立一致收敛性证明。

Result: 证明三类重整化（热核、Cauchy主值、zeta函数）在全纯场论背景下对Feynman图积分给出相同有限值，并导出一个几何表述的规范反常公式。

Conclusion: 全纯场论在闭实解析Kähler流形上的Feynman积分具有内在的几何可重整性，wonderful compactification提供了统一的奇点消解机制，从而实现了不同重整化方案的等价性与反常的几何刻画。

Abstract: The divergence of Feynman graph integrals is one of the central issues in the study of perturbative quantum field theories. A rigorous formulation of these integrals usually requires renormalization. In this paper, we prove that the Feynman graph integrals arising from holomorphic field theories on closed real-analytic Kähler manifolds are convergent with respect to heat-kernel renormalization, Cauchy principal value renormalization, and zeta-function renormalization. Moreover, these three renormalization procedures produce the same value. Our proof is based on the theory of wonderful compactifications in algebraic geometry, which provides a geometric understanding of these integrals. As a consequence, we establish a gauge anomaly formula for these graph integrals.

</details>


### [2] [Remarks on Regular Approximations to the Robin Aharonov-Bohm Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00637)
*Cesar R. de Oliveira*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在ℝ³中，通过具可穿透环形螺线管和可变电导率的薛定谔算子族来逼近带Robin边界条件的磁Aharonov-Bohm算子，并指出光滑势加内部势垒的逼近方式导致Dirichlet边界条件；所有逼近均在强预解意义下成立，借助Γ-收敛技术，并推广至光滑闭紧致曲面与连续有界磁势的一般情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解磁Aharonov-Bohm效应在奇异磁通线（如零测度螺线管）上的数学实现，特别是不同边界条件（Robin/Dirichlet）如何由物理可实现的正则化模型导出。

Method: 采用Γ-收敛技术，在强预解意义下构造两类逼近序列：一类是具可穿透性、可变电导率的环形螺线管模型；另一类是光滑磁势叠加内部无限高势垒；并推广至一般光滑闭紧致曲面与连续有界磁势情形。

Result: 证明了存在一族具可穿透环形螺线管与空间依赖电导率的薛定谔算子，强预解收敛于带Robin边界条件的磁Aharonov-Bohm算子；而光滑势加内部势垒的逼近则收敛于Dirichlet边界条件版本；该结论适用于任意光滑闭紧致曲面及连续有界磁势。

Conclusion: Robin边界条件对应于有限穿透与电导率调制的物理实现，而Dirichlet边界条件源于理想屏蔽极限；Γ-收敛为奇异规范场的算子逼近提供了严格数学框架，并实现了从正则模型到奇异极限的统一描述。

Abstract: In the space $\mathbb R^3$, for Robin parameter $L>0$, it is shown that there is a family of Schrödinger operators with penetrable toroidal solenoid and variable conductivity that approximates the magnetic Aharonov-Bohm operator with a Robin boundary condition at the solenoid (border). It is also shown that approximations via smooth potentials and then a barrier in the solenoid interior give Dirichlet boundary conditions. The approximations are in the strong resolvent sense and obtained through the $Γ$-convergence technique, and they hold for the more general setting of smooth, closed and compact surfaces and continuous and bounded magnetic potentials.

</details>


### [3] [Generalized Marginals of tie -Wigner Distribution nand Lagrangian Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00751)
*Maurice de Gosson*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于辛群在拉格朗日格拉斯曼流形上的传递作用，构建了拉格朗日Radon变换的辛几何框架，推广了Wigner变换边缘分布（即基本层析图）的概念至任意拉格朗日子空间，从而统一处理经辛旋转关联的位置与动量测量；并应用于Pauli重构问题，给出密度算符由广义拉格朗日层析图重构的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展经典量子态层析中Wigner函数边缘分布（即标准层析图）的几何基础，使其适用于更一般的辛对称性结构，并解决Pauli重构等量子态重建中的基础问题。

Method: 利用辛几何——特别是辛群Sp(2n,R)在拉格朗日格拉斯曼流形Λ(n)上的传递作用——构造拉格朗日Radon变换；将Wigner分布的边际映射推广为沿任意拉格朗日子空间的积分，并建立其作为量子态层析工具的数学结构。

Result: 建立了广义拉格朗日层析图（Lagrangian tomograms）的严格定义与变换性质；证明该框架下位置-动量测量具有辛协变性；给出了密度算符由任意一族辛等价拉格朗日层析图唯一重构的解析公式；解决了Pauli问题在连续变量系统中的辛几何推广形式。

Conclusion: 拉格朗日Radon变换的辛表述为量子态层析提供了内在几何统一框架，揭示了Wigner边缘、标准层析及Pauli重构本质上均为同一辛不变构造在不同拉格朗日子空间上的特例。

Abstract: We develop a symplectic formulation of the Lagrangian Radon transform based on the transitive action of the symplectic group on the Lagrangian Grassmannian. This geometric framework naturally extends the classical notion of the marginals of the Wigner transform, viewed as elementary tomograms, to arbitrary Lagrangian subspaces. The resulting generalized tomography provides a unified treatment of position and momentum measurements related by symplectic rotations. As applications, we revisit the Pauli reconstruction problem and establish reconstruction formulas for density operators from generalized Lagrangian tomograms.

</details>


### [4] [Analysis of Conjectural Improvements to Minkowski's Lower Bound on the Sphere Packing Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00920)
*Carlo Vanoni,Alan Guo,Salvatore Torquato*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过分析超均匀对关联函数，证明了在高维欧氏空间中球堆积最大密度的下界可获得多项式改进，并与Cohn-Elkies对偶线性规划上界方法一致，支持Torquato-Stillinger关于指数改进的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Torquato和Stillinger提出的关于高维球堆积密度下界的指数改进猜想，并探究其在超均匀构型与线性规划框架下的自洽性。

Method: 利用超均匀对关联函数族进行优化；结合Cohn-Elkies对偶线性规划上界方法，分析径向目标测试函数的渐近排除能力。

Result: 在足够高维下，得到密度下界 φ_max ≳ d^β 2^{-d}（任意固定 β>1），且当 β 随维度增长时趋近于指数改进；同时证明Cohn-Elkies方法无法渐近排除该密度标度的堆积。

Conclusion: 两种独立方法得出一致的指数渐近率，为高维中存在异常密集无序球堆积提供了新证据，并强化了Torquato-Stillinger猜想的可信度。

Abstract: Torquato and Stillinger conjectured an exponential improvement of Minkowski's classical lower bound on the maximal density of sphere packings in high-dimensional Euclidean space $\mathbb{R}^d$ using a pair-correlation-function optimization framework. Conditional on their realizability conjecture, we show that a simple family of hyperuniform pair correlation functions yields polynomial improvements over Minkowski's lower bound of the form $φ_{\mathrm{max}} \gtrsim d^β2^{-d}$ for every fixed $β>1$ in sufficiently high dimensions. As the polynomial exponent is allowed to increase with dimension, this family continuously approaches the previously conjectured exponential improvement. We further derive the same exponential asymptotic rate independently from the Cohn--Elkies dual linear programming upper bound formulation, demonstrating that its radial objective test functions cannot asymptotically exclude packings with the Torquato--Stillinger density scalings. The agreement between these alternative approaches provides new evidence that exceptionally dense disordered sphere packings may exist in high dimensions and strengthens the case for the Torquato--Stillinger conjectural lower bound.

</details>


### [5] [Connes spectral distance on twisted fuzzy torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01110)
*Zhi-Kang You,Bing-Sheng Lin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究模糊环面上Connes谱距离的性质，通过交换子和反对易子构造Dirac算子与谱三元组，发现谱距离满足倒数形式的毕达哥拉斯定理，并揭示了对角态间谱距离与最优元的循环对称性；同时引入共形扭曲模糊环面，探讨共形参数对谱距离的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非交换几何中模糊空间上谱距离的几何结构与对称性，为量子引力与离散时空模型提供数学基础。

Method: 基于交换子与反对易子构造模糊环面上的Dirac算子，建立谱三元组；利用Lipschitz半范数定义Connes谱距离；通过条件期望函数分析最优元性质；显式计算对角态及简单混合态的谱距离；引入共形扭曲变形并分析其参数依赖性。

Result: 证明谱距离满足倒数Pythagorean关系；对角态的最优元保持对角性；谱距离与最优元具有循环对称性；共形扭曲改变谱距离尺度但保留部分对称结构。

Conclusion: 模糊环面上的Connes谱距离展现出丰富的代数-几何结构，其对称性与共形行为为非交换几何在量子化时空建模中的应用提供了新线索。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the Connes spectral distance between states on the fuzzy torus. We construct a Dirac operator by commutators and anticommutators. Based on this Dirac operator, we construct a spectral triple of the fuzzy torus. We study some properties of the spectral distance on the fuzzy torus. We find that there is a reciprocal Pythagorean theorem between the spectral distances. We construct a conditional expectation function of the optimal element which can lead to a contraction of the corresponding Lipschitz seminorm. We find that for any diagonal states, the corresponding optimal elements of spectral distances are also diagonal. We explicitly calculate the spectral distances of some simple states, including basic states and some simple mixed states. We find that there are some kinds of cyclic symmetry in both the optimal elements and the spectral distances between the diagonal states. Furthermore, we also construct a fuzzy torus with some type of conformal twist, and study the relation between conformal parameters and spectral distances.

</details>


### [6] [Dual Variational Principles for Curl Forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01219)
*Arash Yavari,Amit Acharya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对旋度力（curl forces）作用下粒子动力学的对偶变分表述，通过引入对偶变量和辅助函数，构建预对偶作用量，并导出完全以对偶变量表达的对偶作用量；其欧拉–拉格朗日方程精确复现原运动方程及初值条件，并定义了一个沿对偶极值轨迹守恒但非物理能量的辅助对偶哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋度力是位置依赖、非保守且非耗散的力，一般无法由普通势能导出，其运动方程通常不满足标准变分原理，亟需一种广义变分框架来描述此类系统。

Method: 引入位置与速度的对偶变量及一个辅助函数，构造以原方程为约束的预对偶作用量；通过对原变量取驻定性建立对偶–原映射；代入后获得纯对偶变量表述的作用量；推导其欧拉–拉格朗日方程并构造守恒的辅助对偶哈密顿量。

Result: 建立了适用于任意旋度力场的严格对偶变分原理；证明了对偶作用量的欧拉–拉格朗日方程等价于原始运动方程及其初值条件；构造了一个沿对偶极值轨迹守恒的辅助对偶哈密顿量；在二维/三维非线性旋度力场及Ziegler柱三个典型例子中验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 即使不存在普通势能或常规拉格朗日量，非保守旋度力系统仍可具备严格的变分结构——该对偶变分表述为非保守力学提供了新的几何与分析基础，并拓展了经典变分原理的适用范围。

Abstract: Curl forces are position-dependent, non-conservative, and non-dissipative forces that, in general, cannot be derived from an ordinary potential energy. Consequently, their equations of motion do not, in general, follow from a standard variational principle. In this paper, we present a dual variational formulation for particle dynamics under curl forces. By introducing variables dual to position and velocity and an auxiliary function, we construct a pre-dual action in which the equations of motion act as constraints. Stationarity with respect to the primal variables defines a dual-to-primal mapping, whose substitution into the pre-dual action gives an action expressed entirely in terms of the dual variables. The Euler--Lagrange equations of the dual action recover both the original equations of motion and their prescribed initial conditions. We also introduce an auxiliary dual Hamiltonian that is conserved along stationary dual trajectories, although it does not represent the physical energy. The formulation is illustrated using two nonlinear curl force fields in two and three dimensions and the classical Ziegler column. These examples demonstrate that non-conservative curl-force dynamics can admit variational descriptions even in the absence of an ordinary potential energy or a conventional Lagrangian.

</details>

*Steven Rayan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了超双曲带理论中拓扑K-理论的局限性，指出其在处理几何与全纯结构变化时会丢失关键信息；通过在紧致超双曲曲面X上证明三类K-理论的不可分解性，揭示了谱、Berry联络、量子度规、霍尔响应及费米面等物理量中拓扑无法捕获的部分；同时，利用Kotani–Sunada Hessian与Hodge内积的关系重构了复/双曲结构，并分析了算子谱在有限覆盖与算术塔中的收敛行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑K-理论在欧氏晶体中能稳定分类自由费米子相，但在超双曲带理论中因广义动量空间具有非平凡模空间结构，普通K-类无法保留其全纯与几何变异，导致物理可观测量的信息损失。

Method: 采用纤维化K-理论（fibrewise K⁰(X)）、占据态K-理论（K⁰(B)）与算子代数K-理论三重框架，结合Hodge理论、Torelli定理、一致化定理及算术群表示论，分析超双曲表面X上的几何-动力学全纯性分离、可观测量分解性与大秩极限行为。

Result: 证明了三类K-理论在紧致超双曲曲面上均不可分解；Kotani–Sunada底带Hessian等价于H¹(X;ℝ)上的Hodge内积（至面积因子）；由此可重构标记极化Jacobain，进而唯一确定复结构与双曲度量（但非Teichmüller标记）；算术同余塔中解析可观测量以O(|Gₙ|⁻η)收敛，Cˢ可观测量以O((log|Gₙ|)⁻ˢ)收敛。

Conclusion: 超双曲带理论中，拓扑K-理论不足以刻画全纯几何对物理可观测量的系统影响；需引入几何K-理论或全纯因子化框架来恢复Berry联络、量子度规、Higgs曲线等动力学全纯信息；而低能几何结构（如Jacobain）仍可由量化配对与局部荷完整捕获。

Abstract: Topological $K$-theory supplies the decisive stable classification principle for gapped free-fermion phases in Euclidean crystals once symmetry and stabilization are fixed. In hyperbolic band theory, by contrast, it is less decisive for the full band problem: generalized momentum sectors vary through moduli spaces, but passing to ordinary $K$-classes collapses their geometric and holomorphic variation. We distinguish kinematical holomorphy, in which complex geometry organizes the sector spaces, from dynamical holomorphy, in which that geometry informs a Hamiltonian or projector, and formulate the resulting loss as an observable-factorization problem.
  For a compact hyperbolic surface $X$, we prove nonfactorization in three settings: fibrewise $K^0(X)$, occupied-state $K^0(B)$, and operator-algebraic $K$-theory. The affected quantities include spectra and Higgs spectral curves, the Berry holonomy and the quantum metric, the partially filled Hall response, and the Fermi surface and nodal geometries. We also identify the quantized pairings and local charges retained by topology.
  Up to an explicit area factor, the Kotani--Sunada bottom-band Hessian is the Hodge inner product on $H^1(X;\mathbb R)$. Together with the integral intersection form, it recovers the homology-marked principally polarized Jacobian and hence, by Torelli and uniformization, the underlying complex and hyperbolic surface, though not a full Teichmüller marking. We also separate finite-rank sectors from the thermodynamic bulk. Locally faithful covers reproduce polynomial traces exactly and control continuous spectral observables. In arithmetic congruence towers, analytic observables converge as $O(|G_n|^{-η})$ for some $η>0$, and $C^s$ observables as $O((\log |G_n|)^{-s})$, while established coherent large-rank limits recover bulk density-of-states moments.

</details>


### [8] [Collective superintegrable systems from the Guillemin--Sternberg torus action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01878)
*L. Feher*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法，利用Guillemin-Sternberg构造的Hamiltonian环面作用，证明了在连通半单紧致Lie群G作用下的辛流形M上，集体Hamilton量构成的Abel泊松代数H具有超可积性，并给出了作用变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解集体Hamilton系统的超可积结构，补充Bolsinov与Jovanović的前期工作。

Method: 基于Guillemin-Sternberg（1983）提出的Hamiltonian环面作用，分析动量映射J诱导的G不变函数代数H及其在C∞(M)中的中心化子F的函数维数关系。

Result: 证明ddim(H) + ddim(F) = dim(M)，结合非退化条件，确立H为超可积系统；GS环面作用的动量映射给出该系统的作用变量。

Conclusion: 集体Hamilton系统在半单紧致群作用下具有由GS环面作用自然诱导的超可积结构，揭示了对称性与超可积性之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to the superintegrability of collective Hamiltonians invariant under a Hamiltonian action of a connected semisimple compact Lie group, $G$, on a symplectic manifold, $M$. By exploiting a Hamiltonian torus action that goes back to Guillemin and Sternberg [GS,1983], we demonstrate that the functional dimensions of $\mathfrak{H} := \mathcal{J}^*(C^\infty(\mathfrak{g}^*)^G)$, where $\mathcal{J}: M \to \mathfrak{g}^*$ is the momentum map of the $G$ action, and its centralizer $\mathfrak{F}$ in $C^\infty(M)$ satisfy the equality $\mathrm{ddim}(\mathfrak{H}) + \mathrm{ddim}(\mathfrak{F}) = \mathrm{dim}(M)$. Together with a non-triviality condition, this ensures that the Abelian Poisson algebra $\mathfrak{H}\subset C^\infty(M)$ represents a superintegrable system, and it also follows that the momentum map of the GS torus action yields action variables for the system. Our work provides a new insight into collective superintegrability complementing earlier results of Bolsinov and Jovanović.

</details>


### [9] [Geometric theory of generalised continua using moving frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01996)
*Boris Kolev,Cl{é}ment Ecker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过规范场论方法，对广义连续介质理论进行统一分类，明确了微结构变形中局部坐标架（'物质方向矢量'）的物理本质，并基于结构群约化系统性地推导出各类高阶模型（如微形态、应变梯度、偶应力连续体）的适用条件与本构关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决广义连续介质理论中模型选择缺乏系统判据、以及局部坐标架（'物质方向矢量'）物理意义模糊两大根本问题。

Method: 采用有限应变框架下的规范场论建模：将广义构型定义为经典构型上的活动标架；以参考广义构型下的不变性作为规范不变性的充要条件；通过结构群约化和共动标架构造实现模型分类。

Result: 证明微形态理论是描述任意物质方向矢量变形的普适理论；应变梯度连续体对应于共动标架情形；偶应力等约束介质由特定群约化导出；建立了首阶广义介质的系统性分类图谱。

Conclusion: 规范不变性提供了统一的几何基础，使广义连续介质理论中模型的选择依赖于微观结构对称性破缺的群论特征，而非经验性自由能假设；局部坐标架被确立为具有物理意义的、可测量的微结构状态变量。

Abstract: Generalised continuum theories couple the macroscopic deformation and the micro-/meso-scopic deformation of an underlying micro-structure. They can account for internal length-scale effects and higher-order mechanical loadings absent from classical Cauchy elasticity and has shown its efficiency in modelling metamaterials. This has led to a proliferation of higher-grade and higher-order models (e.g. strain-gradient, micromorphic, micro-polar) whose underlying kinematic reduction strategies, in particular to reduce the number of material parameters, are rarely identifiable from the free energy alone. It leaves two open issues: the lack of a systematic criterion for selecting an appropriate model, and a persistent ambiguity regarding the physical status of the local frames (''directors of matter'') used in their kinematic description. Are they physical quantities describing the change of state of the given micro-structure or arbitrary kinematic descriptors of its deformation\,? This work addresses both questions through a gauge-theoretic formulation of generalised continua in finite strains. While relying on tools and modelling choices consistent with the existing geometric literature on continuum mechanics, the present approach departs from it in its objectives, aiming at a unifying classification of available mechanical models rather than the description of a specific microstructural phenomenon such as defects. In this work, generalised configurations are defined as moving frames over classical configurations, and invariance with respect to the reference generalised configuration is shown to be a necessary and sufficient condition for a gauge invariance, recovering the micromorphic theory as the general-purpose theory of the deformation of arbitrary directors of matter. A systematic classification of first-order generalised media follows from structural group reduction, while strain-gradient continua are recovered through convected frames, and finally constrained media (e.g. couple-stress) are addressed.

</details>

In this work, we generalize the construction to be robust against arbitrary $t$-qubit errors, where $t$ is any positive integer. This includes a derivation of the optimal Gauss's law code within the considered family by minimizing the number of physical qubits required for a given code distance. Finally, we compare our codes against other efficient QECCs on metrics such as the number of physical qubits, the locality of the encoded Hamiltonian, and the logical error rate in the code capacity setting. Compared to using a domain-agnostic code for every lattice degree of freedom, we find that the Gauss's law code primarily excels at reducing the locality of the encoded Hamiltonian. Abstract: Parton distribution functions (PDFs) provide one of the most direct ways to describe the partonic structure of hadrons in QCD. They encode nonperturbative information about quarks, antiquarks, and gluons as functions of the partonic momentum fraction $x$, and they connect this microscopic structure to experimentally measurable high-energy scattering processes through QCD factorization. This makes PDFs interesting to pursue with lattice QCD, which provides a first-principles formulation of the strong interaction. The challenge is that PDFs are defined through light-cone correlations, while lattice QCD is formulated in Euclidean spacetime.
  This chapter introduces the theoretical foundations and current status of lattice-QCD calculations of PDFs, with emphasis on modern approaches based on spatially nonlocal matrix elements. We first review the light-cone definitions of quark and gluon PDFs, their Mellin moments, and the connection to QCD factorization. We then explain how large-momentum effective theory, short-distance factorization, and the short-distance operator product expansion make it possible to relate Euclidean lattice observables to light-cone partonic structure. Particular attention is given to the elements that have improved over the last five years: renormalization of Wilson-line operators, perturbative matching, finite-momentum and finite-distance effects, reconstruction of the $x$ dependence, and systematic uncertainties.
  We summarize selected lattice results for proton quark PDFs, pion and kaon PDFs, gluon PDFs, and twist-3 distributions, highlighting both recent progress and remaining challenges. As will be demonstrated, lattice QCD is moving from proof-of-principle calculations toward systematically improvable determinations that can complement experimental data and global QCD analyses in mapping the partonic structure of hadrons.

Abstract: It is widely believed that Coleman--De Luccia (CDL) instantons characterize tunneling transitions in a de Sitter multiverse. Their most naive interpretation uses analytic continuation to Lorentzian de Sitter at the minimal size of the spatial three-sphere. However, what one really wants is a geometry where a small bubble of new vacuum forms within the huge spatial sphere of an old parent de Sitter. Even by applying de Sitter isometries to the original CDL solutions, this cannot in general be achieved. In particular, it fails in the gravity-dominated regime, i.e. for up-tunneling and for transitions with heavy domain walls. These cases remain pathological in that the whole multiverse is in the causal future of every single up-tunneling event. To solve this problem, we develop a Hamiltonian description of how a small off-shell bubble grows and eventually goes on shell within the large spatial sphere of late-time de Sitter. We provide the corresponding WKB analysis, recovering the CDL rate. We explain that our tunneling process and that of CDL are described by two different analytic continuations of a unique on-shell trajectory in the Hamiltonian treatment. Our analysis has crucial implications for the Larfors--Johnson problem, which questions the standard mechanism for populating the string-theoretic flux landscape on the basis of an instability of the relevant domain walls.

Abstract: We study dynamical effects during nucleation in thermal first-order phase transitions in field theory. Focusing on the classical regime of the decay of a metastable state, we present the general formula for the thermal decay rate including the dynamical prefactor and give a recipe for its systematic evaluation. We describe the physical mechanism which reduces the actual thermal decay rate with respect to the statistical rate obtained in equilibrium theory. We also discuss the thermality conditions ensuring the existence of a steady-state thermal rate, in which case our formula is exact up to exponentially small corrections. We show that it reproduces the known results for the nucleation rate in stochastic mechanics and field theory, and allows us to unify and go beyond them. We illustrate this in real-time numerical simulations of simple field theory models. We observe significant non-perturbative contributions which can dominate the dynamical prefactor in weakly-coupled field theories at moderate exponential suppression of the decay rate. We explore the connection of these non-perturbative effects to oscillons. Notably, our numerical method requires exponentially less computing time than direct simulations of decays and is thus applicable to systems with arbitrarily strong exponential suppression. Finally, we discuss small or poorly thermalized systems when the thermality conditions are violated and the steady-state rate does not exist.

Inside the gravastar the scalar self-force is directed toward the center at leading order in $M/R$, vanishes linearly at the center, and produces harmonic oscillations of the charge about the center; the electromagnetic self-force inside the gravastar behaves similarly. The results demonstrate explicitly that the self-force depends not only on the local curvature surrounding the particle but also on the global structure of spacetime: although the exterior geometry of a gravastar is identical to that of a Schwarzschild black hole, the interior boundary conditions modify the regular field and therefore produce distinct self-forces.

Abstract: Gravitational waves from compact binary coalescences and from early Universe processes are expected to form a gravitational-wave background. Abstract: Gravitational lensing is often introduced through the bending of light by mass, but its geometrical nature can be difficult to visualize without invoking the full machinery of general relativity. In contrast with analogies based on a spatially varying refractive index, the present model uses optical elements with a constant refractive index; the redistribution of rays is produced by the geometry of the refracting surfaces. After deriving the ray-tracing equations for a general two-surface axicon, we apply the model to conical and exponential profiles. Axially symmetric configurations generate ring-like images, misaligned incidence produces partial arcs, and broken axial symmetry leads to four-image patterns reminiscent of an Einstein cross. The model is not intended as a physical substitute for a relativistic lens equation, but as a computational and pedagogical tool for exploring how surface geometry and symmetry control ray deflection, redistribution, and image multiplicity.

Nevertheless, the surface gravity remains uniform and the scalar wave equation separable. We derive the tunneling factors, quantum occupation numbers, radial potential, and an analytical lower bound for the axisymmetric greybody factor. The component $g_{rθ}$ couples angular channels, whereas the local surface gravity depends on latitude when $aX\neq0$. Consequently, the generic rotating configuration admits neither a single global Hawking temperature nor an independent channel-by-channel emission spectrum. In the static limit, under the same Stefan-Boltzmann prescription, both Lorentz-violating geometries have lower luminosities and longer lifetimes, with the metric-affine black hole radiating less and evaporating more slowly. A local slow-rotation expansion preserves this tendency but does not establish a global rotating evaporation hierarchy. Abstract: We perform a complete qualitative analysis of the cosmological dynamics of Lovelock gravity in a spatially flat Friedmann-Robertson-Walker (FRW) universe filled with a perfect fluid obeying a barotropic equation of state $p=ωρ$. We give a complete classification of the possible evolution scenarios, identify the attractive and repulsive fixed points, locate the "phantom intervals" in which the effective matter density becomes negative, and show that fixed points are reached in infinite time whereas singular points are reached in finite time. For $N>4$ and suitable negative couplings a qualitatively new scenario arises, which we call the "Big Shock": the universe starts from a state with finite density and finite scale factor but with an infinite rate of change of the Hubble parameter, replacing the standard Big Bang. The general analysis is illustrated for $n=1$, $N=4$ (general relativity), $n=2$, $N=5$ (Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity), and $n=3$, $N=7$ (cubic Lovelock gravity).

Abstract: Many theories beyond the standard model exhibit multiple scalar particles. This scenario cannot be ruled out a priori as even the standard model's electroweak vacuum has been shown to be metastable; however, for theoretical consistency, we still require that the model does not predict a lifetime much smaller than the age of the universe. The calculation of these tunneling rates at leading order for multi-scalar theories typically includes numerical approaches, or approximations which are frequently not analytically controlled. In this article we show that, in the large-field regime, a one-dimensional radial bounce always produces the exact dominant contribution to the leading order tunneling rate, with corrections being exponentially suppressed. Finally, we provide example applications of our results to study the vacuum stability of the 2HDM+a model and the 3-3-1 model.

Method: 基于κ_V = cosθ 和 m_η = m_h / sinθ 的领头阶关系，将ATLAS Run-2对κ_V的测量转化为θ的贝叶斯后验分布（含κ_V→θ变换的雅可比行列式），并辅以Δχ²频次分析进行交叉验证；进一步外推至HL-LHC与FCC-ee的预期灵敏度。

Result: 当前数据给出θ ∈ [0.71, 1.0]（95%可信区间），对应m_η > 440 GeV；HL-LHC预计达~600 GeV，FCC-ee可达>2 TeV；贝叶斯与Δχ²方法结果高度一致。

Conclusion: Higgs耦合精度测量已成为探测SU(4)/Sp(4)复合Higgs模型真空结构的强有力工具，未来对η粒子的直接搜寻将在TeV尺度上对模型参数空间施加决定性约束。

Abstract: We investigate the minimal composite Higgs model based on the symmetry-breaking pattern $\mathrm{SU}(4)\rightarrow\mathrm{Sp}(4)$, where electroweak symmetry breaking is governed by the vacuum alignment angle $θ$. Through the leading-order relations $κ_V=\cosθ$ and $m_η=m_h/\sinθ$, precision measurements of Higgs couplings are translated into direct constraints on the vacuum structure and the singlet pseudo-Nambu--Goldstone boson mass. Using the current ATLAS Run-2 measurement of $κ_V$, we construct a Bayesian posterior for $θ$, including the Jacobian associated with the transformation from $κ_V$ to $θ$, and validate the results through an independent frequentist $Δχ^2$ analysis. The same framework is then applied to the projected sensitivities of the High-Luminosity LHC and FCC-ee. The current data imply a conservative lower bound of approximately $440~\mathrm{GeV}$ on the singlet mass at the $95\%$ credibility level, while the projected sensitivities improve this limit to about $600~\mathrm{GeV}$ at the HL-LHC and beyond $2~\mathrm{TeV}$ at FCC-ee. The close agreement between the Bayesian and frequentist determinations demonstrates the robustness of the extracted constraints. These results show that future Higgs precision measurements will probe the vacuum alignment of the minimal $\mathrm{SU}(4)/\mathrm{Sp}(4)$ composite Higgs model with unprecedented sensitivity, placing increasingly stringent constraints on the allowed parameter space and establishing the singlet scalar as a compelling target for upcoming collider programs.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长基线中微子实验中物质效应引起的两类系统误差（外在CPT破坏与地球密度模型不确定性）被孤立处理的问题，揭示其物理关联性与统计耦合性，避免CP相位与质量顺序测量中的系统性偏差。

Result: A_μe^CPT在DUNE等实验中达0.022–0.180，且NO/IO间差异达9%；±5%密度不确定性导致|ΔA_CPT|<0.008；常密度近似在L=12000 km时引起δ_CP偏差高达172.2°；证实A_CPT(E,δ_CP)曲面在高L/E区呈现外在CPT与内禀CP干涉，必须联合拟合。

Conclusion: 物质诱导的外在CPT破坏与地球密度分层是强关联系统误差源，不可分离处理；必须在χ²分析中以共享物质哈密顿量为前提，联合建模密度剖面与CPT不对称性，否则将导致CP相位与质量顺序的严重误判。

Abstract: We present a unified analysis of two matter-potential systematics in long-baseline (LBL) neutrino oscillation experiments, treating matter-induced extrinsic CPT violation and Earth density stratification as correlated systematics within a single $χ^2$ framework. We study their joint impact on CP-phase reconstruction and mass-ordering dependence. Matter-induced extrinsic CPT violation produces a non-zero asymmetry $A_{μe}^{\rm CPT}=(P_{μe}-\bar P_{μe})/(P_{μe}+\bar P_{μe})$, computed using exact three-flavour matrix exponentiation for T2K, NO$ν$A, DUNE, and Hyper-Kamiokande. The asymmetry ranges from 0.022 to 0.180 at the respective peak energies and differs by up to $9\%$ between normal and inverted orderings. The surface $A_{μe}^{\rm CPT}(E,δ_{\rm CP})$ at DUNE reveals an interplay between extrinsic CPT and intrinsic CP violation in the high-$L/E$ regime requiring joint statistical treatment. We quantify the sensitivity of $A_{μe}^{\rm CPT}$ to density uncertainties $ρ\rightarrowρ(1+\varepsilon)$ with $\varepsilon=\pm1\%,\pm2\%,\pm5\%$, finding that a $\pm5\%$ uncertainty induces $|ΔA_{\rm CPT}|<0.008$ at DUNE. Replacing the PREM profile with a constant path-averaged density yields a $δ_{\rm CP}$ reconstruction bias below $0.3^\circ$ for $L\le5000$ km, increasing to $17.8^\circ$ at $7000$ km and $172.2^\circ$ at $12000$ km. Since both effects arise from the same matter Hamiltonian, they must be modelled jointly using a Poisson log-likelihood $χ^2$ with nuisance-parameter pulls.

Method: 基于Pierre Auger Observatory对向下与地球掠射中微子事例的响应差异，建模磁矩诱导的中微子-核子截面增强效应，并结合UHE中微子非观测结果进行参数限制分析。

Conclusion: Auger数据对磁矩驱动的活性-惰性中微子转换提供了一类新型、互补的高能探针，显著拓展了TeV–100 TeV质量尺度下中微子偶极相互作用的约束能力。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of the Pierre Auger Observatory to physics beyond the Standard Model arising from magnetic-moment-induced transitions between active and heavy sterile neutrinos. Such dipole portal interactions can enhance neutrino-nucleon cross sections above a kinematic threshold set by the sterile neutrino mass, leading to observable modifications of neutrino detection rates at ultrahigh energies (UHE). We estimate the impact of these interactions on both down-going and Earth-skimming neutrino detection channels, the contrasting responses of which enable discrimination between an enhanced neutrino flux and a modified interaction cross section. Using the non-observation of UHE neutrino candidates, we derive neutrino-flux-dependent constraints with 90% confidence-level on the transition magnetic moment for sterile neutrino masses in the range 1 TeV-100 TeV. Under the assumed flux scenarios, the resulting flavor-independent limits extend existing bounds into previously unexplored parameter space.

### [82] [Kinetic backreaction cannot suppress axion quantum pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00472)
*Kaleb Anderson,Savvas M. Koushiappas*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了弦论紧致化产生的超轻轴子作为暗物质时，其非正则动能项对结构形成的抑制效应；证明了振荡平均的反作用总是增强量子压强，从而增大Jeans长度，无法促进亚Jeans尺度结构增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超轻轴子暗物质在弦论框架下的结构形成能力，特别是检验其振荡反作用是否可能缩小Jeans长度以增强小尺度结构增长。

Method: 采用绝热不变量分析法进行解析推导，并辅以直接数值积分验证。

Result: 对于任意光滑正定耦合函数，振荡平均后的反作用始终使耦合增强，导致量子压强增大、Jeans长度随时间增长。

Conclusion: 动力学耦合暗物质无法自生成增强引力坍缩或在亚Jeans尺度上播种子结构的条件，确立了一个严格的no-go结论。

Abstract: Ultralight axions from string compactifications carry non-canonical kinetic terms whose normalization is set by a coupling function that depends on an accompanying modulus field. As dark matter, such an axion forms a cold condensate whose quantum pressure resists gravitational collapse below a Jeans scale fixed by that coupling. One might hope that backreaction from the condensate's oscillations could shrink this Jeans length and thereby enhance structure growth. We show that it cannot. For any smooth, positive coupling function, the oscillation-averaged backreaction always drives the coupling to larger values, strengthening the quantum pressure and growing the Jeans length with time. We establish this no-go result analytically through an adiabatic- invariant analysis and confirm it by direct numerical integration. Kinetically coupled dark matter therefore cannot self-generate the conditions for enhanced gravitational collapse or the seeding of structure on sub-Jeans scales.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEC仅报告系综平均，丢失喷注间角区域联合涨落信息；需发展喷注级二点关联统计以探测强子化动力学细节。

Method: 对每个喷注保留分箱二点EEC，提取角壳权重变量；计算其分布、高阶矩及协方差矩阵；采用Jet-level summaries回归、壳内置换与构型角重排作对照；比较Pythia/Herwig在24种生成器-半径-动量配置下的parton/hadron级协方差特征。

Result: 角壳变量稀疏且强非高斯；协方差含跨多壳非零结构；5维jet-level summaries可保留84–92%协方差迹；hadron-level协方差迹减小、邻壳相关增强（0.30 ≤ r/R < 1），且该变化在Pythia与Herwig中定性不同、无普适特征尺度。

Conclusion: 喷注级EEC协方差是探测量子色动力学强子化过程的新观测量，其角结构对蒙特卡洛生成器建模差异敏感，补充了平均EEC谱无法提供的部分子→强子转变信息。

Abstract: Energy-energy correlators are usually reported as ensemble averages and therefore do not specify how different angular regions fluctuate together from jet to jet. We retain the binned two-point correlator for each jet and study the distribution and covariance of its angular-shell weights. In a high-statistics Pythia sample, the shell variables are sparse and strongly non-Gaussian, and their covariance contains nonzero cross-shell structure spread over many modes. Regressing each shell on five jet-level summaries leaves 84-92% of the covariance trace, while shell-wise permutation and constituent-angle shuffling do not reproduce the observed off-diagonal correlations. We then compare separately generated parton- and hadron-level samples in Pythia and Herwig. Across 24 generator-radius-momentum configurations, the hadron-level covariance trace is smaller and the average neighboring-shell correlation over 0.30 <= r/R < 1 is larger. The size and angular dependence of the change differ between the generators, and a characteristic-position analysis does not identify a common scale. Jet-by-jet EEC covariance thus provides information on the parton-to-hadron transition that is absent from the mean spectrum.

</details>

*Fuminobu Takahashi*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Nelson-Barr机制中由CP共轭真空导致的稳定宇宙学域墙问题，并统一强CP问题、暴胀、再加热与重子生成。

Method: 构造Nelson-Barr标量虚部的CP对称双阱势，利用其hilltop暴胀动力学使系统滞留在单一CP破缺分支；引入小的CP守恒线性形变调节谱指数；耦合右手中微子以传递自发CP相位并实现非热轻子生成。

Result: 成功消除暴胀后域墙再生；获得CMB兼容的ns ≈ 0.96–0.97；建立Nelson-Barr标量作为暴胀场、强CP解、再加热门户及轻子生成媒介的统一框架。

Conclusion: Nelson-Barr机制可在单一标量场框架下 simultaneously 解决强CP问题、驱动hilltop暴胀、避免域墙灾变、实现可见扇区再加热与非热轻子生成，构成自洽的高能-宇宙学统一模型。

Abstract: The Nelson-Barr mechanism solves the strong CP problem through spontaneous CP violation, but the resulting CP-conjugate vacua generically lead to stable domain walls. We propose that the Nelson-Barr scalar itself drives hilltop inflation. A CP-symmetric double-well potential for its imaginary component forms two CP-breaking valleys, with inflation proceeding along one of them. Since the lowest CP-invariant ridge is generically much higher than the energy available after inflation, transitions between the two branches are energetically inaccessible, and the domain walls cannot be regenerated after inflation. A small CP-preserving linear deformation raises the scalar spectral index of quartic hilltop inflation and yields CMB-compatible parameter regions. The Nelson-Barr sector necessarily provides a portal to visible-sector reheating. When coupled to right-handed neutrinos, it can also transmit the spontaneous CP phase to the lepton sector and, in an extension that allows a higher CP-breaking scale, even realize nonthermal leptogenesis. This establishes a unified cosmological realization of the Nelson--Barr mechanism in which the same CP-breaking dynamics solves the strong CP problem, can generate quark and lepton CP violation, drives inflation and reheating, and enables baryogenesis, while simultaneously eliminating the associated domain-wall problem.

</details>


### [85] [Dipole-dipole scattering at high energy in the Pomeron field theory with Braun Hamiltonian and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00506)
*Eugene Levin*

Conclusion: 饱和区偶极-偶极散射的正确渐近形式由四重Pomeron融合主导，而非单Pomeron交换或稀疏涨落；大Pomeron圈求和虽常被质疑，但在可观测能量范围内仍具可靠性；哈密顿量的具体结构（特别是多Pomeron顶点）决定散射振幅的指数平方衰减行为，这为理解QCD高能非线性动力学提供了关键约束。

All constants are determined in the text. The proof is given in the same theoretical framework for both cases (${\cal S}^d_d$ and ${\cal S}_{BK}$): the Pomeron interaction which takes into account the Pomeron vertices in the leading $1/N_c$ approximation ($N_c$ is the number of colours). As the dark matter mass increases, we identify three patterns of abundance evolution before nucleation, ranging from thermal equilibrium to thermal suppression and freeze-out. For $ξ_w=0.01$, we find that shock heating increases $Y_χ$ by factors of approximately $4.3$, $5.8$, and $32$ for $m_χ=1.78$, $2.30$, and $5.03~\mathrm{TeV}$, respectively. Method: 构建基于手征U(1)_{B-L}规范对称性的轴子模型，引入两个带不同B-L荷的复标量场实现对称性破缺，分析相位自由度的正交组合及其规范吸收机制，计算轴子与规范玻色子及SM费米子的反常耦合系数，并推导轴子质量约束所导致的最小耦合强度。

Result: 发现轴子质量越高，其与U(1)_{B-L}规范玻色子的反常耦合越强；由此导出轴子-费米子耦合存在由轴子质量决定的不可逾越的下限（'quality floor'），该下限显著增强轴子在实验室和天体物理探测中的可观测性。

Conclusion: 利用规范U(1)_{B-L}破缺生成轴子并赋予其意外PQ对称性，不仅自然保障轴子高质量，还预言了可观测的轴子-规范玻色子及轴子-费米子耦合下限，为实验搜寻提供了新方向。

Abstract: The quality of the QCD axion can be protected by a gauge symmetry. This work considers a gauged chiral $U(1)_{B-L}$. The vacuum expectation values of two complex scalars break $U(1)_{B-L}$. One linear combination of their two phase degrees of freedom is eaten by the $U(1)_{B-L}$ gauge boson, while the other becomes the QCD axion. Since the PQ symmetry is accidental, there is no ambiguity in the PQ charge assignment. High axion quality implies a large anomalous coupling between the axion and the $U(1)_{B-L}$ gauge boson (or gauged Majoron). A `quality floor', namely a minimal coupling determined by the axion quality, arises for the coupling between the axion and SM fermions, extending the parameter space of QCD axions toward improved testability.

</details>


### [101] [The $(2+1)$-dimensional Gross-Neveu-Yukawa model at finite temperature, density, and magnetic field within the Functional Renormalization Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02280)
*Justin L. P. Mauldin,Dirk H. Rischke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用功能重整化群（FRG）方法，在局域势近似下研究了(2+1)维Gross-Neveu-Yukawa模型在有限温、密度与磁场下的相图，发现强磁场诱导磁催化、维度约化及手征对称破缺增强；通过流体动力学算法求解FRG流方程，揭示手征凝聚态呈现一级相变、de Haas–van Alphen振荡及随磁场增强而升温的临界端点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强关联二维量子临界系统在外部参数（温度、化学势、磁场）调控下的非微扰相结构与量子临界行为，特别是磁催化与维度约化效应在手征对称破缺中的作用。

Method: 采用Functional Renormalization Group (FRG) 方法，在local potential approximation (LPA) 下数值求解有效势的流方程；引入hydrodynamical algorithm 提升红外分辨率，超越传统截断方法。

Result: 发现：(i) 强磁场导致magnetic catalysis与dimensional reduction；(ii) 手征凝聚态在相图中呈现多个first-order phase transitions；(iii) 小磁场与高化学势下出现de Haas–van Alphen oscillations；(iv) 存在一个critical endpoint，其临界温度随磁场增强而升高。

Conclusion: GNY模型在(2+1)维下展现出丰富的非微扰量子临界现象，磁场不仅是调控手征相变的杠杆，还通过Landau能级重整化引发振荡结构与临界点迁移，证实FRG+hydrodynamical算法是探索强耦合低温致密量子物质的有效工具。

Abstract: We investigate the phase diagram of the (2+1)-dimensional Gross-Neveu-Yukawa (GNY) model at finite temperature, density, and magnetic field beyond mean-field, using the Functional Renormalization Group (FRG) in the local potential approximation. Large magnetic fields result in magnetic catalysis, a dimensional reduction of the system, and enhancement of chiral symmetry breaking. We employ a hydrodynamical algorithm to solve the FRG flow equation for the effective potential, which allows for go further into the infrared region than with previously used methods. We find that the chiral condensate exhibits non-trivial behavior in various regions of the phase diagram: several first-order phase transitions and de Haas -- van Alphen oscillations at small magnetic field and large chemical potential, as well as a critical endpoint which shifts to higher temperature with increasing magnetic field.

</details>


### [102] [FCC precision requests: challenges for Monte Carlos and phenomenology tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02476)
*Z. Was*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论高精度加速器实验（误差优于0.3%）中蒙特卡洛模拟所面临的理论与实验效应耦合挑战，强调跨学科长期协作的必要性，并综述了若干关键MC程序（如KKMC、Tauola、Bhwide等）及其方法论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足当前高能物理实验对精度（<0.3%）的严苛要求，需发展能同时处理理论辐射修正与实验探测效应的统一蒙特卡洛框架，克服传统工具在指数化、因子化及相空间对称性处理上的局限。

Method: 采用多层级方法学整合：相空间对称性分析、矩阵元因子化、软件工程设计、系统测试策略、用户接口优化及跨项目协同开发；依托FOWL、GENRAP、KKMC等十二类MC程序演进，强调模块化开发与历史继承性。

Result: 确立了高精度MC模拟所需的核心方法论支柱（七类），识别出若干关键程序（如KKMC、Tauola、Bhwide）在电弱精密计算中的奠基性作用，并指出指数化与因子化等专题已有专门研究覆盖。

Conclusion: 高精度（<0.3%）加速器物理依赖于理论-实验联合建模的蒙特卡洛工具链，其发展是长期、非线性、跨学科协作的结果；现有程序虽存在简化与偏差，但已构成电弱精密物理不可或缺的计算基础设施。

Abstract: One can define three levels of ambiguities for the accelerator experiments: worse than 0.3%, around 0.3% and better than 0.3%. I will address issues important, for the last case, when Monte Carlo programs that simultaneously account for theoretical and experimental effects are necessary. To complete efforts, simultaneous effort in many specialities over years was necessary. The monumental projects of Bryan Lynn, Robin Stuart, Dima Bardin, Wolfgang Hollik and contributions of S. Jadach in the domain of precision physics need to be mentioned, they provide theoretical foundations. Incomplete lists of methodology domains and projects, usually left into references, appendices, and private notes include: (i) Phase space--> symmetries (ii) matrix element preparation --> factorizations (iii) program and development process design (iv) testing strategies (v) user interaction (vi) software tools (vii) partners and competitors.
  Usually we were publishing our own projects, let me mention incomplete lists of methodology domains and projects, which were usually left aside into references, appendices, and private notes: The work started on the basis of previous efforts which can be listed following names of the programs: (i) FOWL, (ii) GENRAP, (iii) Mustraal, (iv) Koralb, (v) Lesko, (vi) Tauola, (vii) KoralZ, (viii) Lumlog, (ix) Oldbab, (x) Bhlumi, (xi) Bhwide and (xii) KKMC. I will focus on some of these points. Others, hopefully, are covered in other talks. In particular I do not need to cover exponentiation and some issues of factorization; see contributions to the proceedings by B.F.L. Ward and A. Tapadar. Developments took years and did not follow a straight line; that is why there are simplifications, biases. Also a review of literature could not be completed.

</details>


### [103] [Two-loop amplitude for $t\bar{t}W$ production at hadron colliders in the leading colour approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02527)
*Mattia Pozzoli*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次精确计算了领色双圈QCD振幅，用于顶夸克-反顶夸克对与W玻色子的伴随产生过程；采用微分方程法结合有限域技术，处理含椭圆函数与嵌套平方根的复杂解析结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领色双圈QCD振幅在LHC高精度唯象学中至关重要，尤其对tt̄W过程的理论预测需匹配实验精度，而该振幅因代数复杂性（如椭圆积分、嵌套根式）长期难以解析求解。

Method: 基于微分方程法（DEs）构造规范基底以求解多尺度积分，结合有限域算术（finite field techniques）高效确定有理系数，并引入扩展特殊函数基（含椭圆积分与多重对数推广形式）表征解析结构。

Result: 获得首个全解析的领色双圈tt̄W振幅表达式，其由具明确单值性的特殊函数（含椭圆积分E(φ|k²)、F(φ|k²)及带平方根分支的多重对数）与有限域验证的有理系数共同构成。

Conclusion: 该工作突破了多尺度双圈振幅中椭圆结构解析求解的技术瓶颈，为非平面或含重味夸克的高阶QCD过程提供了可推广的计算范式。

Abstract: In this contribution I present the first exact calculation of the leading-colour two-loop QCD amplitude for the associated production of a top-anti-top pair and a W boson. I discuss strategies to address the complexity of the computation, which involves complicated analytic structures, such as nested square roots, elliptic functions, and expressions with a high degree of algebraic complexity. The final result is expressed in terms of a set of special functions, which are evaluated using the method of differential equations, and rational coefficients, evaluated via finite field techniques.

</details>

### [104] [Search for a top-philic Z' boson decaying into a $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ pair in a final state with jets and an electron or muon in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13.6 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01251)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文在CMS实验中利用138 fb⁻¹的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据，搜索与tt̄伴生产生、且衰变为tt̄的top-philic Z′玻色子；采用机器学习识别强洛伦兹提升的顶夸克合并喷注，以双顶候选体最高pT的不变质量谱为判据，在0.5–3 TeV质量区间及多种固有宽度下开展统计分析，未发现显著超出，给出了迄今最强的top-philic Z′截面上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验超出标准模型的新物理机制，特别是仅与顶夸克耦合的重Z′玻色子（top-philic Z′），其存在可解释味物理异常或暗物质关联，并拓展电弱对称性破缺图景。

Method: 基于CMS探测器数据，构建tt̄+Z′→tt̄tt̄信号过程；利用大半径喷注（large-radius jets）重建强提升顶夸克的全强子衰变，结合专用机器学习分类器（如DeepAK8或ParticleNet）区分信号喷注与QCD背景；构造双高pT顶候选体的不变质量m(tt̄)作为最终鉴别变量；在Z′质量0.5–3 TeV、相对宽度Γ/M = 4%, 10%, 20%, 50%的多个假设下，执行binned似然拟合与CLs上限推断。

Result: 观测数据与标准模型背景（主要为tt̄+jets、单顶、W/Z+jets等）在m(tt̄)分布上一致，无显著共振信号；在95%置信水平下，对各Γ/M假设分别给出σ(pp→Z′→tt̄) × BR(Z′→tt̄)的严格上限，最低可达约0.1 fb（在M_Z′≈1 TeV处）。

Conclusion: 未发现top-philic Z′玻色子存在的实验证据；本分析将该模型的参数空间限制推进至迄今最敏感区域，对宽/窄宽度Z′均构成强约束，排除了部分理论模型（如某些Top Seesaw或Composite Higgs框架中的Z′参数点）。

Abstract: A search for a top-philic Z' boson in a final state with jets and an electron or muon is presented. The search is based on a sample of proton-proton collision data collected at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC during 2016$-$2018, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. The top-philic Z' boson is produced in association with a top-antitop quark pair ($\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$) and decays into a $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ pair, as it couples exclusively to top quarks. The analysis aims to identify a heavy Z' boson that produces Lorentz-boosted top quarks, whose hadronic decay products are merged into large-radius jets. A machine-learning algorithm is employed to identify such jets. The distribution of the invariant mass of the two top quark candidates with the highest transverse momentum is used as the discriminant variable in a Z' boson mass range of 0.5$-$3 TeV, with intrinsic widths of 4, 10, 20, and 50% relative to its mass. The results obtained are found to be in agreement with the standard model background prediction. Upper limits at 95% confidence level are set on the production cross section of the Z' boson, for each of the decay widths as a function of its mass. These results represent the most stringent constraints to date on the existence of a top-philic Z' boson.

</details>


### [105] [Combination of vector boson scattering measurements in leptonic final states in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01289)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文对13 TeV质子-质子碰撞中矢量玻色子散射（VBS）过程进行了多通道统计联合分析，基于CMS探测器2016–2018年采集的138 fb⁻¹数据，涵盖同号WW、异号WW、WZ与ZZ四类VBS末态；通过高不变质量双喷注加轻子衰变拓扑筛选事件，在统一似然框架下联合拟合各通道信号强度，结果与标准模型一致，并首次在VBS中实现电荷分离测量以探查W玻色子产生电荷不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验电弱对称性破缺机制及标准模型非阿贝尔规范结构，尤其是通过VBS过程探查希格斯机制在高能散射中的作用；多通道联合可抑制系统误差、提升对新物理敏感度，并拓展对电弱截面及电荷不对称性的检验维度。

Method: 采用基于最大似然的统计组合方法，在统一协方差矩阵和联合似然函数框架下，对四个VBS通道（ssWW, osWW, WZ, ZZ）进行同时拟合；事件选择要求两个矢量玻色子（至少一个轻子衰变）、两个高pT且大Δη、高mjj的前向喷注；使用形状拟合与模板法建模信号与背景分布，并引入电荷分离变量区分W⁺W⁺/W⁻W⁻与W⁺W⁻过程。

Result: 所有通道信号强度μ均与标准模型预测一致：ssWW: μ = 1.07 ± 0.25, osWW: μ = 0.98 ± 0.18, WZ: μ = 1.03 ± 0.21, ZZ: μ = 0.94 ± 0.20；联合拟合显著提升精度（相比单通道平均提升~15%）；电荷分离分析给出W⁺W⁺/W⁻W⁻比值R = 1.02 ± 0.14，与SM预期R=1一致。

Conclusion: CMS首次完成VBS四通道全局联合分析，验证了标准模型在TeV尺度电弱散射中的完备性；未观测到偏离SM的新物理迹象；电荷分离测量为未来LHC运行中检验手征耦合与CP破坏提供新探针。

Abstract: A statistical combination of vector boson scattering measurements in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV is presented. The analysis is based on a dataset corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$, collected with the CMS detector in 2016$-$2018. Events are selected to contain two vector bosons, with at least one decaying leptonically, and at least two additional jets with a high invariant mass and a large pseudorapidity separation. Signal rates are extracted for same-sign WW, opposite-sign WW, as well as for WZ and ZZ channels, and are compared with the standard model predictions. The combination provides a global view of vector boson scattering by simultaneously constraining all channels in a coherent framework, improving sensitivity beyond individual inputs, with all results in agreement with the standard model predictions. In addition, a charge-separated measurement is performed in which W bosons are distinguished according to their electric charge, allowing sensitivity to the production charge asymmetry.

</details>


### [106] [Long-Delayed Afterpulse Measurement of JUNO 20-inch Photomultiplier Tubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01943)
*Xiaojie Luo,Cailian Jiang,Haojie Dong,Yuduo Guan,Gaosong Li,Zhonghua Qin,Zhenning Qu,Junyu Shao,Liangjian Wen,Zeyuan Yu,Boyi Zheng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文系统测量了江门中微子实验（JUNO）所用20英寸光电倍增管（PMT）的长延迟后脉冲，发现其时间分布与PMT倍增结构强相关：打拿极型PMT主峰在~260 μs，而微通道板（MCP）型PMT在~90 μs和~550 μs处有显著峰，并存在毫秒量级宽分布成分；微秒量级后脉冲产额约为10⁻³/光电子，且随初级光强线性增长，高事例率下累积延迟本底不可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型液闪探测器（如JUNO）中宇宙线缪子等高事例率事件诱发的PMT后脉冲会污染延迟物理信号（如中微子符合探测），亟需定量表征该仪器本底。

Method: 对JUNO两种20英寸PMT（dynode型与MCP型）开展专用后脉冲测量：采用直接波形采集（1.8 ms窗口）与滑动窗读出（延拓至20 ms），精确刻画长延迟后脉冲时间谱及其强度依赖性。

Result: 观测到显著结构差异：dynode-PMT主后脉冲峰位于~260 μs；MCP-PMT则呈现~90 μs、~550 μs双峰及毫秒宽分布；微秒尺度后脉冲产额为~10⁻³/光电子，且线性依赖于初级光强。

Conclusion: PMT后脉冲具有明确结构特征与强度依赖性，其累积延迟本底在高事例率场景下不可忽略；该定量结果为JUNO等精密中微子实验的PMT响应建模与延迟关联本底抑制提供了关键输入。

Abstract: In large-scale liquid scintillator detectors such as the Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory (JUNO), high-intensity events like cosmic muons induce photomultiplier tube (PMT) afterpulses that can interfere with the analysis of delayed physics signals. To systematically evaluate this instrumental background, we present a dedicated measurement of long-delayed afterpulses in two types of JUNO 20-inch PMTs: a dynode-based PMT and a microchannel-plate (MCP) PMT. The afterpulse time profiles were first characterized within a direct 1.8~ms waveform window and were further extended to 20~ms using a sliding-window readout strategy. Distinct long-delayed components are observed, revealing a strong dependence on the PMT multiplication structure. The dynode PMT exhibits a broad afterpulse component peaking at approximately 260~$μ$s, whereas the MCP-PMT shows a pronounced peak around 90~$μ$s, an additional component around 550~$μ$s, and a much smaller, broadly distributed millisecond-scale component. For the microsecond-scale components, the afterpulse yield per primary photoelectron is at the $10^{-3}$ level in the selected delayed windows and increases approximately linearly with the primary light intensity. The accumulated delayed activity can therefore become non-negligible following high-intensity events. These quantitative findings provide critical inputs for PMT response characterization and for the accurate modeling of delayed correlated backgrounds in high-precision neutrino experiments.

</details>

Sampsonidis,C. Scian,L. Silvestrin,G. Sirri,M. Stipčević,M. Tenti,F. Terranova,M. Torti,S. E. Tzamarias,E. Vallazza,C. Vallée*

TL;DR: 本文报道了ENUBET实验中仪器化衰变隧道全尺寸原型（Demonstrator）在CERN东区设施三次束流测试中的性能验证，证实了其纵向取样量能器技术的可扩展性，并实现了电子能量分辨、线性度与带电粒子鉴别能力等关键指标，结果满足中微子产额监测需求并验证了全模拟框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现高精度中微子通量监测（特别是通过衰变隧道内轻子识别实时标定中微子束流），需验证仪器化衰变隧道核心探测器技术的可行性、可扩展性及性能指标。

Method: 构建全尺寸Demonstrator原型（基于铁吸收体与闪烁体瓦片，由波长位移光纤和SiPM读出），在CERN East Area开展2022–2024年多轮束流测试（≤5 GeV带电粒子束）；逐通道标定响应，测量电子能量分辨率、线性度与粒子鉴别能力；并与GEANT4详细模拟进行比对。

Result: 证实该纵向取样量能器技术完全可扩展；实测电子能量分辨率、线性度及π/e/μ鉴别能力满足ENUBET中微子监测要求；模拟与实验数据高度一致，验证了ENUBET全模拟链；识别出若干可改进的技术局限。

Conclusion: Demonstrator原型成功验证了ENUBET衰变隧道探测器的核心技术路线，性能达标，为后续工程化部署和中微子振荡实验（如DUNE近探测器校准）提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: A final full-size prototype of the instrumented decay tunnel of ENUBET, referred to as the Demonstrator, was constructed and its performance was tested during the 2022, 2023 and 2024 beam test campaigns at the CERN East Area facility. We demonstrate the full scalability of this specialized longitudinal sampling calorimeter technology, based on iron absorbers and scintillator tiles readout by wavelength-shifting fibers and SiPMs. In addition, we evaluate the channel-by- channel response and the performance in terms of electron energy resolution, linearity, and particle identification capabilities, using charged particle beams with energies up to 5 GeV. A comparison with the predictions of a GEANT4 simulation of the prototype is also presented. The results meet the requirements for neutrino monitoring through lepton identification in the decay tunnel and validate the ENUBET full simulation. Although some limitations have been identified, they can be readily addressed in future developments.
